### 第二批跨平台 fixture 重导资源合同

最终 engine 源码完成增量复审并冻结后，Darwin 复用本树 `services/engine/.venv`，Linux 仅复用 VPS 已有 `/opt/linker-engine/current/.venv/bin/python` 的依赖，实际导出代码来自本批源码。负责人父代理与 `sol_local_retire_implementation`。VPS 仅允许 1 个 `/tmp/linker-planner-fixture-548-storage-lifecycle` 临时目录，装入 engine 源码、tests、schemas、锁文件和必要 fixture 原件，不复制 venv，不读取真实工作区，不监听端口、不启停生产服务。导出通常约 10 秒，执行 `nice -n 19` 并将 BLAS/OpenMP 线程限制为 1，最长 60 秒；成功、失败、超时均由远端 trap 与调用方 finally 精确回收该目录，结束读回目录不存在。本地 exporter 的单份合成 SQLite/CAS 由既有 TemporaryDirectory 退出回收。结果仅覆盖现有两平台 fixture 和 manifest，不新增副本；这些小型合同证据随对应源码保留，后续源码变更时原位重导替换。磁盘本轮读回可用 841 GiB。

跨平台重导结果：engine 增量独立复审无阻塞后，Darwin 与真实 Linux exporter 完成；相对各自上一版均仅 100 个 64hex 身份/派生哈希叶变化，非身份字段变化 0。canonical sorted JSON SHA-256：Darwin `6c2086a3cd75906c49b60851261ef00c6ff9e85bb82840947060365151fb1985`；Linux `050f264a951b9f69c3ec572781c02c6c2c4ae4bed021449dfebcda1372d36529`。只执行完整 Darwin exporter 复现、两平台 manifest、跨平台显式差异 mask 三项合同，3 项通过（10.99 秒）；未重跑未变化单元。Linux 传输包为 442 个源码/必要夹具文件、压缩 1,666,056 bytes，未复制 venv；远端目录与 exporter 临时合成 workspace 均已回收，读回确认远端目录不存在。初次直接调用因缺 tests 导入路径失败；初次 Linux 合成任务因缺间接使用的 PVGIS 原件失败，均在补齐原有导入路径/只读夹具后解决，未修改产品源码或依赖。

CAS 校验的明确边界：计划和恢复点校验拒绝非空 WAL，要求先安全停写并 checkpoint；不会使用 immutable 忽略已提交 WAL 事务。无 WAL 的静态数据库通过 immutable 连接真正只读，回归核验未新增辅助文件、已有文件内容和 mtime 不变。GC apply 由配对服务维护窗口停写后重算。

### 第二批候选与交叉评审结论

Planner CAS回收和本地实例退役已完成Sol/high互补实现及交叉Spec/Standards评审。增量评审发现并修复：恢复点DB/manifest符号链接及硬链接不独立；SQLite只读连接仍写WAL/SHM；显式递归CAS子引用缺失；超时后GC子进程存活；先停PHP阻塞依赖Woo的drain；失败marker使退役无法追加新证据决策；preserve父子路径重叠；WordPress实际代码未列证据；cutover与pull load官方入口绕过实例生命周期锁。有效证据和共享symlink目标保持保护。无真实实例或生产CAS删除。

验证复用：Planner相关archive/operator回归及最后3条进程组回归通过，原实现整包mypy356文件及最终增量mypy/Ruff通过；local最终整包mypy42、cutover24、pull22（均含tests）及Ruff通过；local/cutover/pull新增有效回归24项，snapshot18项。两平台真实exporter仅100个身份hash字段变化、非身份字段0，完整复现/manifest/mask三合同通过。最终交叉评审无未处理阻塞；这些是候选验证，不冒称已部署。

## 方法注记

`ProductEvidenceChanged` 的 12 个 raise 点共用一个错误码，引擎又丢弃 message。
本轮据此连续做出 6 个错误推断（主机负载 → manifest 重建 → 旧 manifest →
LiteSpeed 缓存 → 兼容合同 → 平台夹具）。正确顺序是**先复现、再读代码**。

另需注意：`sources/saved-solution` 是**网关层路径**（`Gateway.php:302`），
经 `Transport` 直连引擎会得到 `not_found`，看起来像功能坏了，实际不是。

## Turnstile 的准确状态（更正早前的描述）

早前记录写作「Turnstile 拦截询盘」，这个说法**不准确**。实测后的事实：

| 项 | 实测值 |
| --- | --- |
| `Configuration::active_mode()` | `enforce` |
| `ready()` / `configured()` | `true` / `true` |
| `test_keys` | `false`（真实密钥，非测试密钥） |
| `/proforma-request/` 页面 | `200`，**页面内没有 Turnstile 脚本** |
| 验证发生的位置 | 提交时，服务端调 `Protection/Turnstile.php` 的 siteverify |

也就是说：

- **页面不被拦截**。询盘页正常渲染。
- **保护在提交时生效**，且配置正确、使用真实密钥。
- 我的自动化验收拿不到有效客户端 token，因此**提交被正确拒绝**。

这属于「保护按设计工作，自动化客户端不被允许」，不是功能缺陷，也不是「外部条件导致功能未完成」。

真实询盘与 Genie 提交需要**人工在浏览器中完成**——这正是该保护的用途。用自动化绕过它去换取验收通过，是错误的做法，本线没有这样做。

### 一处自我更正

排查过程中我 grep 到两个 `pow`，一度认为存在 proof-of-work 机制。实际是
`/product-category/accessories/backup-power/` 里的子串，属误匹配，已推翻。

### 完整机制（从 `protection.js` 与生产页面读出）

我早先 grep「`/proforma-request/` 页面里没有 Turnstile 脚本」，这句本身没错，
但据此得出的「被拦截」结论是错的。真实设计是**交互触发、异步加载**：

1. 页面加载时**不**注入 `challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js`；
2. 用户第一次 `focusin` 或 `pointerdown` 表单时，`warm()` 才异步加载脚本并渲染 widget；
3. 提交时走两步：服务端 `POST /wp-json/linker/v1/protection/prepare` 换取 sitekey，
   客户端取得 token，再 `POST …/verify` 换取有时效的 proof；
4. `linker_proof` 隐藏字段由此填充，服务端再调 Cloudflare siteverify。

生产实测确认这套链路已注入：

```
linkerPublicProtectionConfig = {
  "api": "https://de-linker.com/wp-json/linker/v1/protection",
  "nonce": "754057849d",
  "mode": "enforce",
  "businessVerification": "required",
  "language": "en"
}
```

`protection.js` 在页面中出现 3 次（不同区块各挂一次）。

因此：**自动化客户端不触发交互、拿不到 token，提交被正确拒绝。**
这不是缺陷，也不需要「修复」。人工在浏览器中打开询盘页、聚焦表单，widget 会正常出现。

### 受保护动作清单与两种挂载方式

服务端 `Configuration::verification_required()` 对以下动作全部返回 `true`：

```
inquiry      required=yes
contact      required=yes
draft_write  required=yes
genie        required=yes
```

`Protection/Actions.php` 另列 `genie_contact`、`genie_inquiry`；
`Protection/BusinessPolicy.php` 把 `contact`、`inquiry`、`genie_contact`、
`genie_inquiry`、`conversation_mail` 归为业务验证类。

**两种挂载方式，同一套 proof 机制：**

| 入口 | 取 proof 的方式 |
| --- | --- |
| 询盘、联系、草稿写 | `FormFields.php:33` 渲染 `linker_proof` 隐藏字段，`protection.js` 提交前填充 |
| Genie | React 组件调 `window.linkerPublicProtection.proof('genie', widget)`，不走表单字段 |

实测：`/genie/` 与 `/de/genie/` 均注入 `linkerPublicProtectionConfig`（`mode: enforce`），
但页面中**没有** `data-linker-protected-action` 属性——这是 React 挂载方式的正常表现，
不是保护缺失。

因此「Genie 与询盘的失败提示不同」不表示两套机制，只是同一个 proof 在两种界面上
的呈现差异；根因一致：都需要真人交互产生 token。

## 客户侧报告 PDF 下载：生产上无样本，未验证

报告的 **API 内容**已逐行读回（英德各 13 行，见前文）。但**客户拿到的 PDF 文件**
走的是另一条路径，本轮未能验证。

### 机制

`Planning/ReportDownload.php` 注册：

```
GET /wp-json/linker/v1/inquiries/{id}/planning-report/{handoff}?key=...
```

- `{handoff}` 是 32 位十六进制 id，存在订单元数据 `_linker_planning_projects` 里
  （`Context::META`），由 `Context::stored()` 按 `valid_reference()` 过滤后返回。
- 权限三重之一：`manage_woocommerce`、订单客户本人、或持订单 `order_key`。
- 内容由 `linker_tools_planning_report_content` 过滤器供给，默认返回 503。

### 生产实测

扫描全部订单的 `Context::stored()`：

```
orders with report contexts: 0
```

**没有任何订单持有可下载的报告 handoff。** 因此该下载路径在生产上从未被真实使用过，
本轮无法读回一个真实的客户 PDF。

### 一处差点误判（记录下来避免重犯）

我先查了 `_linker_planning_handoff`，发现 10 个询盘全无，一度准备据此写
「客户报告从未交付」。但那实际是 `DraftTransfer::LINE_META`——**购物车行**上的
方案草稿转移标记，与报告 handoff 是完全不同的东西。元数据键相似不等于语义相同。

正确的键是 `_linker_planning_projects`；用它查出的结论才是上面那个。

### 解除条件

需要一个持有报告 handoff 的询盘。产生方式：客户在配置器里生成报告后
把方案发成询盘，`Context` 才会写入 contexts。本轮已有的询盘都早于该能力，
或未走这条路径。

### 权限层已实测（2026-10-31）

下载路径的权限与拒绝行为已在生产验证，均为只读探测：

| 请求 | 结果 |
| --- | --- |
| 无 `key`、无登录会话 | `404 planning_report_not_found` |
| 持该订单有效 `order_key`、但无对应 handoff | `404 planning_report_not_found` |

两次返回**同一个** 404，不区分「你没权限」与「报告不存在」——报告的存在性因此不会
通过该端点泄露，这是正确行为。

### 仍未验证的部分

**一次成功的 PDF 下载。** 需要一份真实持有 handoff 的询盘；生产上 `Context::stored()`
对所有订单均为空。

生产上要产生 handoff，只能由 `DraftTransfer::apply()` 在真实会话中写入，
而它需要引擎签发的完整回执。**我不会伪造这个回执**——伪造出来的「验收通过」
不是客户路径，等于自欺。

解除条件：客户在配置器里生成报告后把方案发成询盘，`Context::capture()` 才会写入
contexts；届时可用该询盘读回一次真实 PDF。

## 3D 视图：生产实测渲染成功

地图 #548 的一条旧记录写作「Chrome 3D context availability remains unproven」。
现已在生产环境实际验证。

### 实测

项目 `88e747eb…`（`Production Roof Acceptance 1946`，revision 12，24 块组件）：

1. 打开 Roof design 工作区（`#lp-design`）；
2. 视图切换 `Plan / Map / 3D` 中点击 **3D**；
3. 视口渲染出**带透视的屋顶平面**（梯形几何），3D 按钮进入激活态；
4. 页面无报错，未出现降级提示。

代码侧对应实现：`apps/planner/src/components/design/SceneViewport.tsx`
（Three.js / React Three Fiber），并在 `webglcontextlost` 时监听恢复，
失败时渲染「3D rendering is unavailable. The design remains available in the plan
view and numeric properties.」——**降级路径也有文案出口**，不会白屏。

### 边界（不要过度解读）

这次验证的是**渲染可用**，不是：

- 3D 视图的交互完整性（旋转、缩放、选中已在 Plan 与 Map 下验证，3D 下未逐一操作）；
- 像素级视觉验收（工具页不受像素门约束，但也没有做 1440/390 × EN/DE 全矩阵）；
- 在不同 GPU / 浏览器下的兼容性——只在当前环境验证。

### 结论

「3D 不可用」这条旧记录**可以关闭**。地图上应改为：3D 渲染在生产可用，
失败时的降级提示存在；交互与跨环境兼容性未做全矩阵。

### 一处看似漏译，实际不是

设计对象面板显示 `Owned synthetic roof for release acceptance`（英文）。
核对 `DesignEditor.tsx:528`：该名称直接渲染 `{face.name}`，**不经过 `__()`**。

这是正确设计——`face.name` 是用户在项目里给屋顶起的名字，属用户数据。
德语用户自己起的名字，界面不应替他翻译。

## 同意与统计：生产实测，统计在同意前不加载

目标完成标准里点名「同意、统计」。此前没有验收证据，现有生产实测。

### 实测（生产首页 HTML）

**GA 脚本被真正扣住**，不是「先加载后 gating」：

```html
<script type="text/plain" data-cookieyes="cookieyes-analytics"
        id="google_gtagjs-js"
        src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=GT-PLVVHJ2K" async>
```

`type="text/plain"` 的脚本浏览器**不执行**。在 CookieYes 授予分析类同意之前，
GA 连脚本都不会运行。

**默认全部拒绝**：

```js
gtag('consent','default',{"ad_personalization":"denied","ad_storage":"denied",
"ad_user_data":"denied","analytics_storage":"denied",
"functionality_storage":"denied","security_storage": ...})
```

### 实现

`plugins/linker-commerce/includes/Consent/BasicMode.php`：

- `script_loader_tag`（优先级 30）扣住 Google tag，改为 `text/plain`；
- `googlesitekit_consent_defaults` 全部 `denied`；
- `googlesitekit_consent_category_map` 去掉 ads 类目；
- `wp_get_consent_type` 在 CLI 下返回 `optin`；
- 另入队 `assets/consent.js` 处理撤回。

CMP 是 **CookieYes**（`cky-*`），统计是 **GA4 `G-N2TGDM3193`**（容器 `GT-PLVVHJ2K`）。

### 边界

验证的是**页面输出与默认状态**。本次 HTML 抽查未点击同意。另核对 2026-10-19 私有生产 network-cookies.json 的原始七条记录：英德各 1440/390 首访和拒绝的 Google 请求与跟踪 Cookie 均为 0。仅统计、仅访问来源、双同意分别有 2、0、2 条测量相关请求，三种选择的撤回均通过。详见 [既有生产验收](../../2026-10-19/201-consent/README.md)。10 月 28 日的偏好弹窗焦点修改另计；这里没有重跑生产测量，也没有扩大到今天的 GA Realtime。

## 历史：项目修订与计算记录可读

目标完成标准点名「历史」。此前只有零散提及，现补上实测。

### 项目修订历史

项目 `181228648034…` 当前 revision 16，指定读取历史版本时：

```
current revision=16
rev1 readable, sha=58023034feb9
```

`projects/read` 接受 `revision` 参数，早期版本的输入哈希与当前不同，
说明每个 revision 保留独立的输入快照。

### 计算历史（浏览器实测）

Energy calculation → `Saved calculations`（URL 变为 `&energy_view=runs`），
`Calculation history` 列出跨 revision 的记录：

```
Revision 12  Planning report        Report ready            10/30/26, 9:34 PM
Revision 12  Planning report        Report ready            10/30/26, 9:27 PM
Revision 12  Energy calculation     Calculation complete    10/30/26, 9:22 PM
Revision 12  Equipment checks       Equipment check complete 10/30/26, 9:09 PM
Revision 11  Energy calculation     …
```

选中的那条展开为报告预览，含 `Download PDF`、`Download bill of materials (CSV)`
与 `Add selected products to solution draft`。

### 一处看起来不一致，实际是设计

界面语言是英文，历史列表也是英文（`Report ready`），但预览正文是德语
（`Projektplanungsbericht`），且标注 `Report language: German`。

核对 `PlanningReportResult.tsx:170-176`：正文语言由 `report.locale` 决定，
并在与界面语言不同时显示该提示（`reportLanguageDiffers`）。选中的报告本身
生成于德语环境，因此正文是德语——**这是正确行为**，不是语言串台。

### 边界

未验证：删除或重命名历史记录、超过一页时的分页、以及跨项目的记录隔离。

### Knowledge identity/bounded batch PHP resource contract

Owner: sol_planner_gc_implementation; branch `codex/knowledge-identity-batches`, reused tree `/Users/chesterjohn/Documents/de-linker-worktrees/unified-planner-548`. Scope: native Woo identity authority, authenticated lease-bound batches, knowledge claim/receipt fencing and their PHP tests. Source/test files remain with the capability and are maintained there; no new worktree, site, dependency install, production request or source copy. One existing PHP test process at a time; synthetic fixture temporary trees use one enclosing task temporary directory and existing test teardown, then finally remove that directory on success or failure. No persistent test server/process or extra report. Existing INDEX evidence from other owners is preserved. Production epoch 44/source 817 remains immutable.

The API uses independent `model_identity_revision` and preserves published `source_revision`. Private `texts` on the existing identify endpoint is bounded to 20 original windows of 16,000 UTF-8 bytes, requires authenticated exact knowledge lease ownership, and returns only matched labels plus an opaque identity hash. Old knowledge clients/receipts without the new protocol/identity cannot establish verified readiness.


PHP identity/batch candidate validation (local only): `Publication` unit directory 61 tests / 556 assertions; existing `EngineeringFeedTest` 20 / 130; affected `EngineeringReferenceTest` 10 / 47. Full `linker-commerce` PHPStan (includes tests/fixtures) passes; WPCS passes all nine changed PHP source/support/test files. The native batch fixture executes the real dispatcher/queue lock with current Woo-shaped relationships, proves 20 independent windows share one fresh map with no PDF access, rejects cross-window phrases/auth/budget/lease mismatches, and keeps unknown legacy identities from readiness. The draft-only fixture changes an unavailable model label while published source and engineering feed stay byte-equivalent after removing generated time; knowledge validation/reconcile/ACK reject the previous identity. Existing engine `Publication.read` selects known fields; `EngineeringFeed::feed` explicitly projects its strict schema, so identity is absent from feed. No engine schema expansion is needed.

Upgrade boundary: an old HTTP knowledge worker missing `identity_protocol=native-model-identification-v1` receives 409 before leasing; old or mismatched loaded identity cannot grant current knowledge readiness. `/validate` keeps identity optional for unchanged engineering consumers, so a PHP-only rollout cannot retrofit the old Genie runtime's proof rules. This batch requires the new Genie runtime (legacy index remains diagnostic-readable but grants no passages under the new runtime), PHP authority and new worker/index in a coordinated release. The reviewed deployment order below supersedes the earlier runtime-first suggestion: freeze the naturally idle old scheduler under its existing lock; deploy PHP/MU while the old runtime and old index retain their original published-source behavior; then the new worker takes one native lease, compiles/materializes and uploads/validates its own new-identity generation; only the verified-upload hook switches runtime, followed by fenced activation, actual loaded proof and ACK. No production rollout/readback is claimed here.

PHP resource readback: reused tree is 7.2 GiB including pre-existing dependencies/evidence; filesystem available 840 GiB at check. This half creates no persistent runtime, dependency copy or production request. All PHPUnit enclosing task temporary trees and generated fixture uploads were removed by teardown/finally; no PHP check process remains after completion. Temporary released bytes were not separately measured; only persistent source/test and this existing INDEX addition remain.

Its AS runner defaults25actions/30s estimated queue budget/concurrent-batch count1; DB claims atomically select only unclaimed due actions and release normally. PR828 release boundary clarification: native WP planner_contracts checks required_input_contracts UNION supported_input_contracts. After new Planner activation, old WP rollback is refused even before plant inputs are saved because its registry lacks pv_plant_string_plan_v1. If no retained revision raises the input floor, use the new Planner operator to restore retained previous first, then native WP rollback. Once a retained revision contains plant inputs, keep a compatible writer and repair forward; never delete inputs or bypass the floor. Normal order remains native Planner prepare/activate/status, then frozen-source cutover deploy; unclear post-switch verification resumes the existing pending deployment rather than making another snapshot/upload. This is source/runbook verification, not a new production observation or deployment.

### WindPress Generate resolved — 2026-11-01

Root cause verified against live LiteSpeed 7.9.2 source and production options: `cache-login_cookie` was incorrectly set to WordPress `LOGGED_IN_COOKIE`. LiteSpeed `Vary::_update_vary_name()` selected this name and `_cookie()` wrote its 32-character MD5 vary value over the native WordPress root authentication cookie. The secure-admin cookie remained valid, explaining why wp-admin worked while authenticated REST returned `rest_cookie_invalid_nonce`. A fresh nonce matched the next request but could not repair the malformed root cookie. Core api-fetch recursively refreshed/retried it. This is an evidenced extra request-load source; it does not establish the cause or proportion of historical Hostinger CPU above 90 percent.

After verified full incremental backup above, SSH re-entry failed at banner exchange twice and was stopped. Root used the user's existing authenticated wp-admin session and LiteSpeed's native Cache > Advanced form: cleared only Login Cookie and clicked Save Changes. The form showed both a generic could-not-save notice and Options saved; a fresh page reload proved the target field persisted empty. Excludes > Do Not Cache Cookies still contained the original WordPress logged-in cookie name. Toolbox > View .htaccess proved the LOGIN COOKIE/Cache-Vary alias was removed and NOCACHE COOKIES protection remained. No hand-edited rewrite, custom authentication, password/role change or security relaxation was used. The original page-cache evidence and DynamicHtml comment are corrected to prevent reapplication.

Normal same-account sign-in then replaced the damaged login cookie using the user's explicitly supplied original credential file. That file was opened through native TextEdit, closed without saving, never copied, and credential values were cleared after form submission. Earlier attribution to an expired/absent login was incorrect: the real failure was the conflicting cache-cookie configuration.

Actual official Generate completed once after the repair. Persisted rendering mode was Cached (the earlier Hybrid selection had not been the saved mode); no WindPress mode/settings Save was performed. Providers/cache-index/volume and all observed scan requests returned 200. The remaining wait was the official lightningcss WebAssembly download: 15,844,785 decoded bytes, HTTP 200, completed without retry. UI reported `Cache generated in 2m 55.3s.` Native `/wp-json/windpress/v1/admin/settings/cache/store` returned 200 with last_generated 1793502949, last_full_build 1793502928157, file_size 903746, and the official tailwind.css URL. Cache-index readback returned 200.

After navigation away and back, Performance still displayed Last Generated `2026-11-01 11:15:49`, 903.74 kB, Cached. Core authenticated `/wp-json/wp/v2/users/me` returned 200. Actual homepage DOM loaded `/wp-content/uploads/windpress/cache/tailwind.css?ver=1793502949`; browser network returned 200, Last-Modified `Sun, 01 Nov 2026 03:15:49 GMT`, compressed Content-Length 103141. No repeated nonce failure was observed in the repaired build/readback. This closes the official Generate gate; it does not close the paused broader unified goal or its unpublished candidate.

Unique final visual evidence: [WindPress successful persistent cache](windpress-generate-success.png). Network/Runtime diagnostic subscriptions disabled, CUA kernel reset to discard all transient AX/credential/network state. User-owned tab329403464 is left open on the successful Performance page; no agent-created browser tab/window or local runtime was retained. SSH/rsync resources were closed by backup owner. Original credential file and unrelated TextEdit document unchanged. This narrow fix changed only the live cache-cookie setting and generated cache; repository changes are its existing evidence records and a comment-only correction.

Unified goal resumed. Root owns one loopback-only HTTP listener on an OS-assigned port, serving an in-memory render of the actual NetworkViewport with the accepted synthetic endpoint fixture and existing WindPress CSS from the verified snapshot. Lifetime up to 8 minutes, close on completion or failure. One task-owned Chrome tab with temporary 390px viewport, reused for the rendering check and, if available, read-only Hostinger peak-window evidence. Close it and discard viewport after use. No site instance, project, file copy or exported log. Independent platform/CI/Hostinger owners keep separate bounded resource lifetimes.

### Shared hosting peak windows and inquiry receipt

Read-only hPanel traffic tables (one temporary tab, closed) now cover all four sites sharing the account. Values are requests per panel-displayed hour. The order is de-linker, metal-divider, staging.metal-divider, sunriver-inverter. 10-28 04:00 was 927 / 330 / 587 / 145. 10-29 14:00 was 610 / 64 / 0 / 118. 10-30 00:00 was 6,635 / 109 / 0 / 159. 10-30 14:00 was 845 / 16 / 16 / 158. 10-30 15:00 was 1,566 / 345 / 20 / 144. 10-30 16:00 was 2,356 / 144 / 18 / 173. The hour containing the displayed 15:40 CPU peak has 2,075 requests, with 1,566 on de-linker. The different traffic mix at other saturated hours, and the higher de-linker request count in the following hour, make an attribution by request count alone unreliable. hPanel exposes request response time in milliseconds but the access table is capped at 10,000, presents rolling-period/search/sort controls, and does not expose per-request CPU/fault or a custom peak-window CPU breakdown. Clock-zone and bucket semantics remain provider-unconfirmed. No raw client data, log exports or new sampling process persisted.

The verified November 1 recovery point contained 253 HPOS inquiries/orders. The sole order created after the October 22 intake intervention is the authorized acceptance inquiry 8457. Its NORMAL authorization is stored independently of the cleaned transport jobs. Team job 8458 recorded delivery 02:12:01 UTC, receipt job 8459 at 02:12:02. The native managed-mailbox INBOX envelope previously retrieved for UID 1424 at 02:12:01 UTC on Oct 31 has exactly the same Message-ID as the team witness in that snapshot, including original formatting. Root compared both in RAM and emitted only match flags. Inquiry ID, purpose and message hash also agree inside the witness. The two contemporaneous transport ledger entries lack a per-job foreign key, so temporal proximity alone does not assign a specific ledger entry to each message. This proves the authorized inquiry's team reception. The 38 quarantined tasks still have zero attempts, and the remaining natural NORMAL inquiry with an actual customer product draft has no candidate in this snapshot. Local process lifecycle candidate 9d997778 fixes actual group cleanup after leader exit and failed/timeout startup. stop waits for all live group members, then escalates TERM to KILL within the existing bounded grace. The common live-leader path uses _alive rather than scanning the host; only a vanished leader requires group/member inspection, ignoring zombies and handling Darwin empty-group EPERM. restart retains its spawn identity even when wait_for_port reaps the leader. A failed/cancelled PID record write also cleans the newly spawned session. Existing PID-reuse guards remain. Timeout/startup exceptions retain this run's diagnostics. Four process lifecycle cases first reproduced the exact live socket leaks, then passed.
